Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any cohomologies module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 757. The projective resolution has length at most 657 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the cohomologies space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 757 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 657. QED.
